```
def _conv(a,b):
    L = 1 << (len(a)+len(b)-2).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,L); return np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)[:len(a)+len(b)-1]
```
For a=b, one FFT. Clip negative tiny. This will be used heavily.

Let's look at the LP direction in SOTA. For fixed f, maximize sum g subject to f*g ≤ M. This finds a response that avoids f's peaks. Then convex combination. Formulation: Given p (sum1), M = max c(p,p). Solve LP: maximize Σ g_j subject to g≥0 and (p*g)_k ≤ M for all k. Why M? Because p*g is bilinear cross-convolution. The optimal g has sum >? ### Fast convolution
Implement `_conv_full(a,b=None)` using rfft.
```
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft  # or np.fft
def _conv_square(a):
   n=len(a); L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
   A=rfft(a, L); return irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
```
Cross gradient for y weights w:
```
def _grad_cross(w, p):
   # w length 2n-1, p length n
   nr = np.concatenate((p[::-1], np.zeros(len(p)-1)))? Use convolution length (2n-1)+n-1=3n-2.
   L = 1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
   W=rfft(w,L); F=rfft(p[::-1],L); conv=irfft(W*F,L)
   return 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Length L for n=3809: 3n-2=11425, next 16384, okay. Memory maybe fine. Could compute gradient with direct for small n.

```
def _lse_y(y, beta):
```

### Full-length smooth optimization

Pseudo:
```
def _mirror_polish(p, deadline, rng, base_eta=0.12, max_iters=1500):
    p=_normalize(p); p=np.maximum(p,1e-12); p/=p.sum()
    n=p.size
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score(p)
    betas=[1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512,1024]
    for beta in betas:
       iters = min(200, max(30, max_iters//len(betas)))
       eta = np.clip(base_eta * (16.0 / beta)**0.5, 0.01, 1.0)
       for it in range(iters):
          if time>deadline-1: break
          c=conv_square(p); y=2.0*n*c
          m=y.max(); z=beta*(y-m); z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
          expz=np.exp(z); w=expz/expz.sum()
          # gradient = 4n cross(w,p)
          g=4.0*n*_conv_cross_weights(w,p)
          # center to reduce scale? not necessary before normalize.
          # adaptive step: maybe line search eta in list [eta, eta/2, eta/4]
          best_p_trial=None; best_soft=soft_current? We can compute current soft = m + log(expz.sum())/beta
          # But computing trial requires conv. Use small fixed with damping by momentum? 
          p = p * np.exp(-eta * g)
          p = np.where(p>0,p,0); p/=p.sum(); p=np.maximum(p,1e-12); p/=p.sum()
          # occasionally evaluate true score for best
       update best every it/10
```

We can just set p and in next iteration compute new score; if high, maybe continue. To avoid divergence, we can cap changes: ensure after update, max p not too large? ### Expansion and finite corrections
`_expand_to_length(proto, target_len)`: same as previous: replace each mass p_i by constant block length proportional. For target_len not multiple, lengths varying 1. This preserves mass. After expansion, the sequence values may not be optimally distributed inside blocks. We can then polish with mirror/coordinate.

Not 1.5. Thus optimal likely uses dense support m≈N to increase average pairs to n/2, reducing relative max.

Maybe related to **perfect 1-error-correcting codes**? Hmm.

- k=2i: (p_i+δ)^2 - p_i^2 = 2p_iδ+δ^2
- k=2j: (p_j-δ)^2 - p_j^2 = -2p_jδ+δ^2

### LP polish implementation details
Need import `scipy.optimize.linprog`, `scipy.sparse`. If unavailable, fallback.
```
def _lp_polish(p, c, score, deadline, max_iters=6, max_n=512):
   # p sum1
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>deadline-20: break
      n=...
      M = max(c) * 1.0
      # Solve LP: max sum g s.t. K g <= M, 0<=g<=UB
      # Use p cleaned; if any p_i==0, constraints less restrictive. To avoid unbounded, UB=10.
      i_col = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)  # g_j affects rows i+j for i=0..n-1, col j
      rows = np.tile(np.arange(n), n)  # i values? For col j, row=i+j.
      data = np.tile(p, n)
      K = sparse.csr_matrix((data, (rows, i_col)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
      res=linprog(c=-ones, A_ub=K, b_ub=M, bounds=[(0,UB)]*n, method='highs')
      if success: g=res.x; g=clip; gs=sum; if gs<=1e-6 break; g/=gs
      cg=conv_square(g); cross=conv(p,g)
      # line search t
      best_t=0; best_score=score
      for t in np.linspace(0,1,41): ...
      if no improve break
      p = (1-t)old+t*g; normalize; c=conv; score=...
   return best
```
Note M in LP uses current c sum1. Since scaling, if score target t_score, M = target/(2n)? Could use M = max(c)*1.05. If M too tight, LP maybe infeasible? At p, Kp = p*p = c ≤ M, and g=p feasible with sum1. So feasible. Bound UB: p max maybe 1, UB=100 enough. If optimum sum large due zeros, cap may cut. But line search still maybe.
We can also solve LP minimizing `M/g_sum`? No.
Try different M_factor: [1.0, 1.1, 0.9]? If factor<1 maybe infeasible even p? p has c max M, so feasible at p for M=current. Use 1.0. Could use 1.2 to allow more mass but p*g larger than current max, line search may still improve. Use list factors [1.0,1.05,1.1] in iterations? The previous used M exactly. We can implement multiple factors per iteration.
The line search objective with precomputed arrays: `ct = aa*cp+2ab*cross+bb*cg`; score=2n max(ct). Choose min. Analytic minimum of vector max of quadratics in t? Could solve but grid fine.
After LP direction, coordinate polish again.

### Potential performance pitfalls

```
def _conv_square(a):
    n=a.size
    if n <= 200:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       L=1 << (2*n-1 -1).bit_length()? Need (2n-1).bit_length()
       A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.clip(c.real,0,None)
    return c
```
If using numpy fft, sufficient. `L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()`? For n=3809, 2n-1=7617, 7616 bit_length 13, 1<<13=8192. `(2*n-1).bit_length()` for 7617 ->13, 1<<13=8192. Good.
- For cross gradient length 3n-2: L=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length() maybe 16384. Fine.
- Memory: 16384 complex arrays small.

### Mirror polish details

```
def _mirror_polish(p, c, score, deadline, rng, max_iters=1000, betas=None, max_eta_step=0.7):
    n=p.size; best=...
    if betas is None: betas=[1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256]
    if n>2000: betas = betas[:6] maybe
    iter_budget = max(50, max_iters // len(betas))
    for beta in betas:
       # ensure c corresponds to p
       if c None: c=conv(p)
       current score compute
       eta_initial = 0.5 / (1.0 + 0.35*np.log2(beta+1))
       for it in range(iter_budget):
          if time > deadline-2: break
          val,w = _soft_assets(c,n,beta)
          g = _grad_y(w,p)
          if not finite: break
          eta = eta_initial
          improved_step=False
          for attempt in range(3):
              p2=_mirror_step(p,g,eta)
              c2=_conv_square(p2)
              val2,_ = _soft_assets(c2,n,beta)
              if val2 < val - 1e-12:
                   p,c,val= p2,c2,val2
                   best_score = min(best_score, 2n max c)
                   improved_step=True
                   break
              else:
                   eta*=0.5
          if not improved_step:
              # try smaller eta maybe 0.1 eta_initial or switch to noise
              if eta_initial>1e-6: eta_initial*=0.5
              else: # perturb and restart beta?
                   break
       # after beta, coordinate? not here
    return best...
```
Line search with LSE may fail if gradient zero or local. We can accept if `val2 <= val + 1e-10`? Good.
`_grad_y(w,p)`: W sum1. Use cross = `_convolve_arrays(w, p[::-1])`? We'll implement `_cross_weights(w,p)`:
```
def _cross_weights(w, p):
    n=p.size
    if n<=200:
       # direct for simplicity
       out=np.empty(n)
       wrev = w
       for i in range(n): out[i]=np.dot(w[i:i+n], p)
       return out
    else:
       L=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
       W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); F=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],L)
       conv=np.fft.irfft(W*F,L)
       return conv[n-1:2*n-1].real
```
Direct loop for n<=200 maybe okay; for n=256 direct 65k per grad, okay; for large FFT.
Then `g = 4.0 * n * cross`.

Step target: Instead of max log change based on max g, we can set `eta = min(eta_initial, 0.5 / (np.max(np.abs(g))+1e-12))`. This caps log change at 0.5. But if gradient scale changes, eta might shrink too much. We'll do:
```
scale = np.max(np.abs(g - np.mean(g)))
if scale>0: eta = min(eta, 0.5/scale)
```
This caps max log change at 0.5. eta_initial maybe 1.0, so effective at most 0.5/scale. If scale ~4, eta .125, logchange .5. Good.
Backtrack halves.

Soft value for beta=1: if y max huge? fine. Use `z = beta*(y-m)`, clip lower -50; upper maybe 50 to avoid exp overflow. But softmax weights with clip upper? If clip upper, shape slightly wrong but stable. Better use `z = beta*(y-m); z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)`; includes upper. Good.

We need compute `c` initial if None. In mirror, if p has zeros, log step underflows; add tiny eps:
```

```

### Let's carefully implement `_coord_update` to avoid bugs.
```
def _coord_update(p, c, i, grid, refine_count, current_score=None):
    n = size; old = float(p[i]); D=1-old
    if not finite: old=0; D=1
    c_k = c[i:i+n].copy()
    coeff = 2.0 * p.copy()
    const = -2.0 * old * p.copy()
    mid = i
    coeff[mid]=0.0
    const[mid] = -old*old
    # unaffected max
    max_unaff=0.0
    if i>0: max_unaff=max(max_unaff, c[:i].max())
    end=i+n
    if end < c.size: max_unaff=max(max_unaff, c[end:].max())
    xs=grid.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isin(gs? just finite, 0<=x<=1)
    add old, 0
    sort unique
    if size==0 return
    # candidate eval
    aff = c_k[None,:] + coeff[None,:]*xs[:,None] + const[None,:]
    aff[:,mid] += xs*xs
    max_aff = aff.max(axis=1)
    maxc = np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S=D+xs
    vals=np.full(len(xs),inf); valid=S>1e-15; vals[valid]=2n*maxc[valid]/(S*S)
    idx=argmin; best_x, best_val
    if best_val >= current_score - tol: return no improve
    # refinement
    if refine_count>0:
       left = xs[idx-1] if idx>0 else 0.0
       right = xs[idx+1] if idx<len(xs)-1 else (best_x*10 if best_x>0 else 1.0)
       if best_x>1e-12:
          local = best_x*np.logspace(-2,2,refine_count)
       else:
          local = np.logspace(-10, np.log10(max(right,1e-12)), refine_count)
       local=unique(concatenate([left,right,best_x,local]))
       # eval similar, update if better
    update
```
Refinement right if idx last and best_x maybe 1.0 -> right=1.0. Use local within [0,1]. Good.
After find x, update:
```
S=D+x
p_new=p/S; p_new[i]=x/S
c_new=c/S^2
aff_best = c_k + coeff*x + const; aff_best[mid]+=x*x
c_new[i:i+n]=aff_best/S^2
# renorm due float
snew=p_new.sum(); p_new/=snew; c_new/=snew^2
# clip nonnegative
score_new = 2n*max(c_new)
if score_new < current_score - tol: return
```
Potential: c includes max 2n-1; c_k slice i:i+n valid because k=i+j (j 0..n-1). Good.
`max_unaff` if c length 2n-1 and end=i+n. For i=0, start0; end=n; unaffected c[n:] length n-1. For i=n-1, c[:-1] unaffected, end=2n-1 no.

### Coordinate cycles recompute c each cycle

### LP bound UB
`UB = 10.0 / n?` Wait g variables can be arbitrary; after normalization, a coordinate with g_j=100 and others0 gives p' delta; possible. But cross constraints with p_i small may allow. UB=100.0 okay. Too large may cause LP ill-conditioned? Use UB=20.0. If p min=1e-6, M maybe 1e-4, g_j can be 200 before infeasible; cap 20 may cut optimum. But normalized g with one coordinate 20 is like mass 1 there; that's allowed. UB=20 enough? If p_i=1e-9, constraint p_i g_j ≤M => g_j≤1e5, but p_j mass capped 1 after normalization? Since g normalized sum1, any coordinate >1 not needed? Actually a coordinate value in g before normalization can be >1, but after normalization it contributes fraction g_j/sum. If one coordinate huge, normalized ~1. Sum large means other small; normalized same as [1,0...]. Cap 10 enough to approximate a delta. Use UB=10.0.
If p has zeros, constraints with p_i=0 do not restrict g_j for some rows? But other nonzero p constrain. If p all zeros impossible. UB 10 safe.

Array cleaning:
```
def _clean_array(x):
    a=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1: a=a.ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0,out=a)
    return a
```
But if input is list of booleans? Clean converts True to 1; final output should not contain bool. Fine.

`_normalize`:

```
a=_clean_array(a)
s=a.sum()

return a/s
```

`_conv_square`:
Direct threshold n<=256? Use np.convolve for small. For larger, rfft. Need handle real.
```
def _conv_square(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
    if n <= 256:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        L=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
        A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
    np.clip(c,0.0,None,out=c)
    return c
```
If n=256, L=512, direct fine. Fourier small maybe faster but okay.

`_score_normalized`:
```
n=p.size; if n<=0 inf; maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else0; return 2.0*n*max(0,maxc)
```

`_score_array`: normalize, conv, score.

`_soft_assets(c,n,beta)`:
```
y=2.0*n*c
m=float(np.max(y))
z=beta*(y-m)
z=np.clip(z,-50.0,50.0)
ex=np.exp(z)
s=float(ex.sum())
if s<=0 or not finite:
    ex=np.ones_like(y, dtype=float)/y.size; w=ex
    val=m
else:
    w=ex/s
    val=m + np.log(s)/beta
return val, w
```
Note y max maybe negative? c nonneg, y nonneg. If beta=0? no.

`_cross_weights(w,p)`:
```
n=p.size
if n <= 300:
    out=np.empty(n,float)
    for i in range(n):
        out[i]=np.dot(w[i:i+n], p)
    return out
else:
    L=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1],L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(W*F,L)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1].real
```
For n=300 direct 90k dot in Python loops may be okay but for mirror many small n maybe use FFT for all? FFT overhead maybe. Use direct for n<=128.
```
if n<=128 direct else FFT.
```

`_mirror_step`:
```
def _mirror_step(p, g, eta):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    g=np.asarray(g,float)
    if not finite g: return p
    g = g - np.mean(g)
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    newlog = logp - eta*g
    newlog = newlog - np.max(newlog)
    new = np.exp(newlog)
    s=new.sum()
    if not finite s or s<=0: return p
    new/=s
    return new
```

`_mirror_polish`: Need p,c,score current. Use deadline.
```
def _mirror_polish(p, c, score, deadline, rng, max_iters=1200):
    n=p.size
    best_p=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score
    betas = [1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256] if n<=1500 else [1,2,4,8,16,32]
    iters_per_beta = max(20, max_iters // len(betas))
    eta_base = 1.0
    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
        # ensure c from p
        c = _conv_square(p); score=_score_normalized(p,c)
        if score < best_score: best=...
        eta = np.clip(1.0 / (1.0 + 0.25*np.log2(beta+1)), 0.05, 1.0)
        improved_beta=False
        for it in range(iters_per_beta):
            if time > deadline -1.5: break
            val,w = _soft_assets(c,n,beta)
            g = 4.0*n*_cross_weights(w,p)
            if not all finite: break
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g - np.mean(g))))
            if scale <= 1e-12: break
            trial_eta = min(eta, 0.6/scale)
            step_ok=False
            for att in range(3):
                p2 = _mirror_step(p,g,trial_eta)
                c2 = _conv_square(p2)
                val2,_ = _soft_assets(c2,n,beta)
                sc2 = _score_normalized(p2,c2)
                if val2 < val - 1e-12:
                    p,c,val = p2,c2,val2; step_ok=True
                    if sc2 < best_score: best=p2.copy(),c2.copy(),sc2
                    if sc2 < score*1.0? no
                    break
                else:
                    trial_eta *= 0.5
            if not step_ok:
                # try with half eta a few? already
                eta *= 0.7
                if eta < 0.01: break
            else:
                improved_beta=True
        if not improved_beta:
            # maybe break or lower beta? since beta increasing, if current failed maybe further harder; break
            break
    # final maybe coordinate not here
    return best_p,best_c,best_score
```
This may break early if no improve at beta=1. But from uniform beta=1 likely improves. If from good local maybe no improve; we could still try coordinate. Good.
Need recompute best initial if p/c from input not current.

Potential issue: LSE value `val` can be negative? y dimensionless 1.5; m+logs/beta positive. fine.

```
def _make_grid(count, min_log=-12.0, max_log=0.0):
```

```
def _lp_polish(p,c,score,deadline,max_iters=4,max_n=512):

       rows = np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
       data = np.tile(p, n)
       K = sparse.csr_matrix((data,(rows,col)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
       UB=10.0
       for factor in (1.0,1.05,1.1):
```

```
def _selective_coordinate_polish(p,c,score,deadline,rng,grid, max_updates=5000):
   n=p.size; best...
   for upd in range(max_updates):
      if time>deadline-2: break
      # choose i
      r=rng.random()
      if r < 0.55:
          i=int(rng.integers(n))
      elif r < 0.85:
          maxc=float(np.max(c))
          active=np.where(c >= maxc*0.97)[0]
          if active.size==0: i=int(rng.integers(n))
          else:
              k=int(active[int(rng.integers(active.size))])
              lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
              i=int(rng.integers(lo, hi+1))
      else:
          top=np.argsort(p)[-50:]  # maybe expensive each time; do every 10
          i=int(top[int(rng.integers(top.size))]) if top.size else 0
      p,c,sc,imp=_coord_update(p,c,i,grid,3,current_score=score)
      if imp: score=sc; if sc<best update
   # final recompute
   p=normalize; c=conv; score=...
   return best
```

### Let's implement `_update_best`
```
def _update_best(state, seq, score, final_len):
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(score): return
    arr=_normalize(seq)
    if arr.size==0: return
    if score < state[1] - 1e-12:
        state[1]=float(score); state[0]=arr.copy()
    if arr.size == final_len and score < state[3] - 1e-12:
        state[3]=float(score); state[2]=arr.copy()
```

```
   mirror_deadline = min(trial_end - 6, time.monotonic() + min(30.0, 0.25*(trial_end-time.monotonic())))
   p,c,sc = _mirror_polish(..., max_iters=500 maybe)
   update
# LP escape
if n <= 512 and time.monotonic() < trial_end - 10:
   lp_deadline = min(trial_end - 6, time.monotonic() + min(45.0, ...))
   p,c,sc = _lp_polish(... max_iters=2)
   update
# final coordinate
if time.monotonic() < trial_end - 1:
   p,c,sc = _coordinate_optimize(..., deadline=trial_end-1, grid, refine)
   update
```

```
def _two_level(n, low_fraction=1.0/3.0):
    m=max(1,n//2)
    p=np.zeros(n)
    p[:m] = (1-low_fraction)/m
    rest=n-m
    if rest>0: p[m:]=low_fraction/rest
    return _normalize(p)
```

```
def _convolve_ab(a,b):
```

```python
"""
Search for negative heights minimizing
    2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)**2).

Algorithm summary
-----------------
The previous state of the art was mainly coordinate line-search on short
prototypes, then block expansion to length 3809.  This program keeps that
structure but adds several different escaping mechanisms:

1. Fast FFT convolution / cross-correlation helpers.
2. Prototype optimization by exact coordinate line search.
3. Hair-tea / LSE gradient descent with multiplicative updates and
   adaptive backtracking.  This smooths the non-smooth max objective and
   can escape local minima found by coordinate descent.
4. A restricted LP direction inspired by the state-of-the-art paper sketch:
   maximize sum(g) subject to p*g <= M, then line-search along p -> (p+g)/2.
5. Multiple diverse initializers, including down-sampled provided sequences,
   sparse supports, Fourier modes, low-center envelopes, and paired
   anti-correlated starts.
6. Expansion of good prototypes to length 3809, followed by full-length
   mirror-polish and selective exact coordinate coordinates.

The returned sequence is nonnegative and has sum 1, so it is safe for the
provided evaluator.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_FINAL_LEN = 3809


def _clean_array(x):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0, out=a)
    return a


def _normalize(a):
    a = _clean_array(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def _conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 256:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        L = 1 << (2 * n - 1).bit_length()
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    return c


def _convolve_ab(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = int(a.size + b.size - 1)
    if out_len <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, b)
    else:
        L = 1 << out_len.bit_length()
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, L)[:out_len]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    return c


def _score_normalized(p, c):
    n = int(np.asarray(p).size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, mx))


def _score_array(seq):
    p = _normalize(seq)
    c = _conv_square(p)
    return _score_normalized(p, c)


def _soft_assets(c, n, beta):
    n = int(n)
    y = 2.0 * float(n) * np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(y)) if y.size else 0.0
    z = float(beta) * (y - m)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    ex = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(ex))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(y.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if w.size:
            w /= w.size
        val = m
    else:
        w = ex / s
        val = m + float(np.log(s)) / float(beta)
    return float(val), w


def _cross_weights(w, p):
    """Return h_i = sum_j w_{i+j} p_j for i=0..n-1."""
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 128:
        out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            out[i] = float(np.dot(w[i:i + n], p))
        return out
    L = 1 << (3 * n - 2).bit_length()
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(W * F, L)
    out = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)


def _mirror_step(p, g, eta):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0 or g.size != p.size:
        return p.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
        return p.copy()
    g = g - float(np.mean(g))
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1.0e-300))
    newlog = logp - float(eta) * g
    newlog = newlog - float(np.max(newlog))
    newp = np.exp(newlog)
    s = float(np.sum(newp))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    newp /= s
    np.clip(newp, 0.0, None, out=newp)
    s2 = float(np.sum(newp))
    if s2 <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s2):
        return p.copy()
    return newp / s2


def _mirror_polish(p, c, score, deadline, rng, max_iters=1200):
    """LSE gradient / multiplicative polish.  Returns best p, c, score."""
    p = _normalize(p)
    c = _conv_square(p)
    score = _score_normalized(p, c)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        p = np.ones(max(1, p.size), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, p.size)
        c = _conv_square(p)
        score = _score_normalized(p, c)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = float(score)

    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1500:
        betas = [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256]
    else:
        betas = [1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]

    iters_per_beta = max(20, int(max_iters) // max(1, len(betas)))

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 2.0:
            break

        p = _normalize(p)
        if np.count_nonzero(p > 1.0e-12) < max(8, n // 4):
            p = p + 1.0e-10 * np.asarray(rng.random(n), dtype=np.float64)
            p = _normalize(p)

        c = _conv_square(p)
        score = _score_normalized(p, c)
        if score < best_score - 1.0e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

        eta = 0.8 / (1.0 + 0.28 * float(np.log2(float(beta) + 1.0)))
        improved_beta = False

        for _ in range(iters_per_beta):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.5:
                break

            val, w = _soft_assets(c, n, beta)
            g = 4.0 * float(n) * _cross_weights(w, p)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
                break

            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g - np.mean(g))))
            if scale <= 1.0e-14:
                break

            trial_eta = min(float(eta), 0.65 / scale)
            step_ok = False

            for _att in range(3):
                p2 = _mirror_step(p, g, trial_eta)
                c2 = _conv_square(p2)
                val2, _w2 = _soft_assets(c2, n, beta)
                sc2 = _score_normalized(p2, c2)

                if (np.isfinite(val2) and val2 <= val + 1.0e-10) or \
                   (np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score - 1.0e-10):
                    p, c, score = p2, c2, sc2
                    step_ok = True
                    if score < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                        best_score = score
                        best_p = p.copy()
                        best_c = c.copy()
                    break
                trial_eta *= 0.5

            if not step_ok:
                eta *= 0.65
                if eta < 1.0e-5:
                    # one last gentle perturbation before abandoning this beta
                    p2 = p * np.exp(np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, n), dtype=np.float64))
                    p2 = _normalize(p2)
                    c2 = _conv_square(p2)
                    sc2 = _score_normalized(p2, c2)
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score - 1.0e-10:
                        p, c, score = p2, c2, sc2
                        if score < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                            best_score = score
                            best_p = p.copy()
                            best_c = c.copy()
                    else:
                        break
                continue

            improved_beta = True

        if not improved_beta:
            break

    return best_p, best_c, best_score


def _make_grid(count, min_log=-10.0, max_log=0.0):
    count = max(3, int(count))
    mn = float(min_log)
    mx = max(mn + 1.0e-4, float(max_log))
    xs = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.logspace(mn, mx, count)))
    xs = xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= 1.0)]
    return np.unique(xs)


def _grid_params(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 32:
        return 60, 22
    if n <= 64:
        return 50, 18
    if n <= 128:
        return 42, 14
    if n <= 256:
        return 34, 10
    if n <= 512:
        return 26, 8
    if n <= 1024:
        return 20, 6
    return 16, 4


def _coord_update(p, c, i, grid, refine_count, current_score):
    """Exact normalized coordinate line-search for one coordinate."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    i = int(i)
    if n <= 0 or i < 0 or i >= n:
        return p, c, current_score, False

    old = float(p[i])
    if not np.isfinite(old) or old < 0.0:
        old = 0.0
    D = 1.0 - old
    if D < 0.0:
        D = 0.0

    c_k = c[i:i + n].copy()
    coeff = 2.0 * p.copy()
    const = -2.0 * old * p.copy()
    mid = i
    coeff[mid] = 0.0
    const[mid] = -old * old

    max_unaff = 0.0
    if i > 0:
        m0 = float(np.max(c[:i]))
        if np.isfinite(m0):
            max_unaff = max(max_unaff, m0)
    end = i + n
    if end < c.size:
        m1 = float(np.max(c[end:]))
        if np.isfinite(m1):
            max_unaff = max(max_unaff, m1)

    xs = np.asarray(grid, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    xs.append(0.0)
    xs.append(old)
    xs = xs[np.isfinite(xs) & (xs >= 0.0) & (xs <= 1.0)]
    xs = np.unique(xs)
    if xs.size == 0:
        return p, c, current_score, False

    aff = c_k[None, :] + coeff[None, :] * xs[:, None] + const[None, :]
    aff[:, mid] += xs * xs
    max_aff = np.max(aff, axis=1)
    maxc = np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff)
    S = D + xs
    vals = np.full(xs.size, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
    valid = S > 1.0e-15
    vals[valid] = (2.0 * n) * maxc[valid] / (S[valid] * S[valid])
    idx = int(np.argmin(vals))
    best_x = float(xs[idx])
    best_val = float(vals[idx])
    tol = 1.0e-13 * max(1.0, float(current_score))

    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val >= float(current_score) - tol:
        return p, c, current_score, False

    if refine_count > 0 and xs.size > 1:
        left = 0.0 if idx == 0 else float(xs[idx - 1])
        right = float(xs[idx]) * 10.0 if idx == xs.size - 1 else float(xs[idx + 1])
        if best_x > 1.0e-12:
            local = best_x * np.logspace(-2.0, 2.0, int(refine_count))
        else:
            up = max(right, 1.0e-12)
            if up > 1.0e-12:
                local = np.logspace(max(-12.0, np.log10(max(left, 1.0e-12))),
                                    np.log10(up),
                                    int(refine_count))
            else:
                local = np.logspace(-8.0, 0.0, int(refine_count))
        local = np.concatenate((local, [left, best_x, right]))
        local = local[np.isfinite(local) & (local >= 0.0) & (local <= 1.0)]
        local = np.unique(local)
        if local.size:
            aff2 = c_k[None, :] + coeff[None, :] * local[:, None] + const[None, :]
            aff2[:, mid] += local * local
            max_aff2 = np.max(aff2, axis=1)
            maxc2 = np.maximum(max_unaff, max_aff2)
            S2 = D + local
            vals2 = np.full(local.size, float("inf"), dtype=np.float64)
            valid2 = S2 > 1.0e-15
            vals2[valid2] = (2.0 * n) * maxc2[valid2] / (S2[valid2] * S2[valid2])
            idx2 = int(np.argmin(vals2))
            if vals2[idx2] < best_val - 1.0e-15:
                best_x = float(local[idx2])
                best_val = float(vals2[idx2])

    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val >= float(current_score) - tol:
        return p, c, current_score, False

    S = D + best_x
    if S <= 1.0e-15:
        return p, c, current_score, False
    S2 = S * S

    p_new = p / S
    p_new[i] = best_x / S
    c_new = c / S2
    aff_best = c_k + coeff * best_x + const
    aff_best[mid] += best_x * best_x
    c_new[i:i + n] = aff_best / S2

    snew = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if not np.isfinite(snew) or snew <= 0.0:
        return p, c, current_score, False
    p_new /= snew
    c_new /= (snew * snew)

    np.clip(p_new, 0.0, None, out=p_new)
    np.clip(c_new, 0.0, None, out=c_new)

    s2 = float(np.sum(p_new))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        return p, c, current_score, False
    p_new /= s2
    c_new /= (s2 * s2)

    score_new = _score_normalized(p_new, c_new)
    if not np.isfinite(score_new) or score_new >= float(current_score) - tol:
        return p, c, current_score, False
    return p_new, c_new, score_new, True


def _coordinate_optimize(p, c, score, deadline, rng, grid, refine_count, max_perturb=3):
    """Cycle exact coordinate updates with occasional multiplicative noise."""
    p = _normalize(p)
    c = _conv_square(p)
    score = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
    n = int(p.size)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = score

    stagnant = 0
    cycles_since_best = 0
    perts = 0
    noise_scales = [0.60, 0.32, 0.16, 0.07]

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 0.4:
        order = np.asarray(rng.permutation(n), dtype=np.int64)
        improved = False
        for i in order:
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 0.2:
                break
            p, c, score, imp = _coord_update(p, c, int(i), grid, refine_count, score)
            if imp:
                improved = True

        p = _normalize(p)
        c = _conv_square(p)
        score = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        tol = 1.0e-12 * max(1.0, best_score)
        if score < best_score - tol:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
            cycles_since_best = 0
        else:
            cycles_since_best += 1

        if improved:
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if score > best_score * 1.25 + 1.0e-8:
                p = best_p.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                score = best_score

        if cycles_since_best >= 4 and score > best_score * 1.02:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            score = best_score
            cycles_since_best = 0

        if stagnant >= 2 and perts < int(max_perturb):
            scale = noise_scales[min(int(perts), len(noise_scales) - 1)]
            p = best_p * np.exp(np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n), dtype=np.float64))
            p = np.maximum(p, 1.0e-12)
            p = p + 1.0e-8 * np.asarray(rng.random(n), dtype=np.float64)
            p = _normalize(p)
            c = _conv_square(p)
            score = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1.0e-10:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
                cycles_since_best = 0
            perts += 1
            stagnant = 0
        elif stagnant >= 6:
            break

    return best_p, best_c, best_score


def _selective_coordinate_polish(p, c, score, deadline, rng, grid, max_updates=4000):
    """Full-length local polish: random/active coordinate exact line-search."""
    p = _normalize(p)
    c = _conv_square(p)
    score = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
    n = int(p.size)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = score

    refine = 3
    top = np.arange(n)
    top_rem = 20

    for _ in range(int(max_updates)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 1.5:
            break

        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.55:
            i = int(rng.integers(n))
        elif r < 0.85:
            maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
            if maxc <= 0.0:
                i = int(rng.integers(n))
            else:
                active = np.where(c >= 0.96 * maxc)[0]
                if active.size == 0:
                    i = int(rng.integers(n))
                else:
                    k = int(active[int(rng.integers(active.size))])
                    lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
                    hi = min(n - 1, k)
                    i = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
        else:
            if top_rem <= 0 or top.size < 20:
                top = np.asarray(rng.choice(n, size=min(n, 80), replace=False), dtype=np.int64)
                top_rem = 20
            else:
                top_rem -= 1
            if top.size:
                i = int(top[int(rng.integers(top.size))])
            else:
                i = int(rng.integers(n))

        p, c, score, imp = _coord_update(p, c, i, grid, refine, score)
        if imp:
            top_rem = 0
            if score < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()

    p = best_p
    c = best_c
    score = best_score
    return p, c, score


def _lp_polish(p, c, score, deadline, max_iters=3, max_n=384):
    """LP direction + line search.  Useful on moderate-length prototypes."""
    try:
        from scipy.optimize import linprog
        from scipy import sparse
    except Exception:
        return p, c, score

    p = _normalize(p)
    c = _conv_square(p)
    score = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 2 or n > int(max_n):
        return p, c, score

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = score

    for _it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 25.0:
            break

        c = _conv_square(p)
        score = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
        if score < best_score - 1.0e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()

        M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        cols = np.repeat(np.arange(n), n)
        rows = np.tile(np.arange(n), n)
        data = np.tile(p, n)
        K = sparse.csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
        obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        UB = 10.0

        got_direction = False
        for factor in (1.0, 1.05, 1.10):
            if time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 15.0:
                break

            b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * float(factor), dtype=np.float64)
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c=obj,
                    A_ub=K,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=[(0.0, UB)] * n,
                    method="highs",
                    options={"presolve": True}
                )
            except Exception:
                continue

            if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
                continue
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            np.clip(g, 0.0, None, out=g)
            gs = float(np.sum(g))
            if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs <= 1.0:
                continue
            g = g / gs

            cg = _conv_square(g)
            cross = _convolve_ab(p, g)

            best_t = 0.0
            best_sc = score
            ts = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 41)
            for t in ts:
                a = float(1.0 - t)
                b = float(t)
                ct = a * a * c + 2.0 * a * b * cross + b * b * cg
                sc = float(_score_normalized(p, ct))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_t = float(t)

            if best_sc >= score - 1.0e-10:
                continue

            lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.25)
            hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.25)
            if hi > lo:
                ts2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 73)
                for t in ts2:
                    a = float(1.0 - t)
                    b = float(t)
                    ct = a * a * c + 2.0 * a * b * cross + b * b * cg
                    sc = float(_score_normalized(p, ct))
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = sc
                        best_t = float(t)

            if best_sc >= score - 1.0e-10:
                continue

            p = (1.0 - best_t) * p + best_t * g
            p = _normalize(p)
            c = _conv_square(p)
            score = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
            if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1.0e-12:
                best_score = score
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
            got_direction = True
            break

        if not got_direction:
            break

    return best_p, best_c, best_score


def _two_level(n, low_fraction=1.0 / 3.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = max(1, n // 2)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:m] = (1.0 - float(low_fraction)) / float(m)
    rest = n - m
    if rest > 0:
        p[m:] = float(low_fraction) / float(rest)
    return _normalize(p)


def _paired_initial(n, rng, alpha_w=1.0, alpha_split=0.3):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = (n + 1) // 2
    weights = np.asarray(rng.dirichlet(np.full(m, float(alpha_w), dtype=np.float64)), dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    split_alpha = max(0.01, float(alpha_split))
    for r in range(m):
        a = r
        b = n - 1 - r
        if a == b:
            p[a] = weights[r]
        else:
            if float(rng.random()) < 0.72:
                if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
                    q0, q1 = 1.0, 0.0
                else:
                    q0, q1 = 0.0, 1.0
            else:
                q = np.asarray(rng.dirichlet(np.full(2, split_alpha, dtype=np.float64)), dtype=np.float64)
                q0, q1 = float(q[0]), float(q[1])
            p[a] = weights[r] * q0
            p[b] = weights[r] * q1
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_initial(n, strategy, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    st = int(strategy) % 20

    if st == 0:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif st == 1:
        p = np.asarray(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)), dtype=np.float64)
    elif st == 2:
        p = np.asarray(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)), dtype=np.float64)
    elif st == 3:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25))
        p = np.asarray(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha, dtype=np.float64)), dtype=np.float64)
    elif st == 4:
        p = np.exp(np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n), dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 5:
        p = np.exp(np.asarray(rng.normal(0.0, float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.55)), n), dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 6:
        p = _paired_initial(n, rng, 0.7, 0.18)
    elif st == 7:
        p = _paired_initial(n, rng, 1.0, 0.80)
    elif st == 8:
        p = _two_level(n, float(rng.choice([1.0 / 3.0, 0.35, 0.25, 0.40])))
    elif st == 9:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.exp(x) * (1.0 + 0.25 * np.asarray(rng.random(n), dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 10:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.exp(-x) * (1.0 + 0.25 * np.asarray(rng.random(n), dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 11:
        p = (np.asarray(rng.random(n) > 0.5, dtype=np.float64)) + 0.08 * np.asarray(rng.random(n), dtype=np.float64)
    elif st == 12:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.35, 0.85))
        p = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.05)
    elif st == 13:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 4.0))
        p = np.power(np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * (x + 0.5) / max(1.0, float(n)))), alpha)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1.0e-4)
    elif st == 14:
        x = np.abs(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) - (n - 1) / 2.0) / (max(1, n) / 2.0)
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.6, 3.0))
        p = np.power(0.25 + x, alpha)
    elif st == 15:
        levels = np.asarray(rng.choice([0.12, 0.45, 1.00], size=n,
                                       p=(0.25, 0.45, 0.30)), dtype=np.float64)
        p = levels * (1.0 + 0.20 * np.asarray(rng.random(n), dtype=np.float64))
    elif st == 16:
        m = max(2, int(n * float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.80))))
        mask = np.asarray(rng.random(n) < (float(m) / float(n)), dtype=bool)
        if int(np.count_nonzero(mask)) < m:
            idx = np.asarray(rng.choice(n, size=min(n, m), replace=False), dtype=np.int64)
            mask[idx] = True
        sup = np.where(mask)[0]
        if sup.size > 0:
            vals = np.asarray(rng.dirichlet(np.ones(sup.size, dtype=np.float64)), dtype=np.float64)
            p = np.full(n, 1.0e-5, dtype=np.float64)
            p[sup] = vals
        else:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif st == 17:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.80))
        p = np.where((np.arange(n) % 2) == 0, 1.0 + amp, 1.0 - amp)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.03)
    elif st == 18:
        sig = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(2, 6))):
            m = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.95))
            sig += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
            if float(rng.random()) < 0.7:
                ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                sig += 0.6 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph2)
        p = np.exp(0.85 * sig)
    else:
        # piecewise sinusoid/exp mixture fallback
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        p = (1.0 + 0.6 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)) * np.exp(-x)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1.0e-4)

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    return _normalize(p)


def _expand_to_length(proto, target_len):
    target_len = int(target_len)
    p = _normalize(proto)
    R = int(p.size)
    if R <= 0 or target_len <= 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, target_len), dtype=np.float64)
    if R == target_len:
        return p.copy()

    if R > target_len:
        out = np.ones(target_len, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(target_len):
            lo = (j * R) // target_len
            hi = max(lo + 1, ((j + 1) * R) // target_len)
            chunk = p[lo:hi]
            out[j] = float(np.sum(chunk)) if chunk.size else 1.0
        return _normalize(out)

    base = target_len // R
    rem = target_len % R
    lengths = np.full(R, base, dtype=np.int64)
    if rem > 0:
        lengths[:rem] += 1
    out = np.zeros(target_len, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = 0
    for i in range(R):
        L = int(lengths[i])
        if L > 0:
            out[idx:idx + L] = float(p[i]) / float(L)
            idx += L
    return _normalize(out)


def _downsample_mass(arr, target):
    target = int(target)
    p = _normalize(arr)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0 or target <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == target:
        return p.copy()
    if n < target:
        return _expand_to_length(p, target)
    out = np.ones(target, dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(target):
        lo = (j * n) // target
        hi = max(lo + 1, ((j + 1) * n) // target)
        chunk = p[lo:hi]
        out[j] = float(np.sum(chunk)) if chunk.size else 1.0
    return _normalize(out)


def _update_best(state, seq, score, final_len):
    """state = [any_seq, any_score, full_seq, full_score]."""
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    arr = _normalize(seq)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    sc = float(score)
    if sc < state[1]:
        state[1] = sc
        state[0] = arr.copy()
    if arr.size == int(final_len) and sc < state[3]:
        state[3] = sc
        state[2] = arr.copy()


def _candidate_key(cand):
    return cand[0]


def _add_candidate(cands, seq, score, limit=32):
    if seq is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    arr = _normalize(seq).copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    cands.append([float(score), int(arr.size), arr])
    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    while len(cands) > int(limit):
        cands.pop()


def _try_full_candidate(cands, seq, score, final_len, limit=24):
    if seq is None:
        return
    arr = _normalize(seq)
    if arr.size != int(final_len):
        if arr.size < int(final_len):
            arr = _expand_to_length(arr, final_len)
        else:
            arr = _downsample_mass(arr, final_len)
        score = _score_array(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    cands.append([float(score), int(final_len), arr.copy()])
    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    while len(cands) > int(limit):
        cands.pop()


def _eval_external(seq):
    seq_list = [float(x) for x in seq]
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq_list))
    except Exception:
        return _score_array(seq_list)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return the best nonnegative coefficient sequence found within budget_s."""
    budget = min(float(budget_s if budget_s is not None else 2400.0), 2400.0)
    reserve = 105.0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, budget - reserve)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    final_len = _FINAL_LEN

    best_state = [None, float("inf"), None, float("inf")]
    proto_cands = []
    full_cands = []

    # Provided previous state of the art, if available.
    hseq = None
    try:
        hseq_obj = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        hseq = _clean_array(hseq_obj)
        if hseq.size > 0:
            sc = _score_array(hseq)
            _update_best(best_state, hseq, sc, final_len)
            _add_candidate(proto_cands, hseq, sc)
            _try_full_candidate(full_cands, hseq, sc, final_len)
    except Exception:
        hseq = None

    # Guaranteed baselines.
    base2 = _two_level(final_len)
    sc = _score_array(base2)
    _update_best(best_state, base2, sc, final_len)
    _try_full_candidate(full_cands, base2, sc, final_len)

    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
        if best_state[0] is None:
            best_state[0] = base2
        return [float(x) for x in _normalize(best_state[0])]

    print(f"initial best={best_state[1]:.8f} full={best_state[3]:.8f}", flush=True)

    # ---------------- Prototype phase ----------------
    # Lengths get increasing budgets.  Prototypes are expanded later.
    proto_specs = [
        (8, 1.0), (12, 1.2), (16, 1.6), (24, 2.2), (32, 3.0),
        (48, 4.5), (64, 6.5), (96, 9.0), (128, 12.0),
        (192, 16.0), (256, 22.0), (384, 30.0), (512, 36.0),
        (768, 42.0), (1024, 48.0)
    ]
    raw_sum = float(sum(w for _, w in proto_specs))
    proto_budget = max(220.0, 0.56 * max(1.0, budget - reserve - 140.0))
    scale = proto_budget / max(1.0, raw_sum)
    proto_deadline = min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + proto_budget)

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    for n_proto, weight in proto_specs:
        if time.monotonic() >= proto_deadline:
            break
        n_proto = int(n_proto)
        assign = max(3.0, float(weight) * scale)
        trial_slice = max(4.0, min(45.0, assign / 6.0))
        grid_count, refine_count = _grid_params(n_proto)
        grid = _make_grid(grid_count, -11.0, 0.0)

        t_end = min(proto_deadline, time.monotonic() + assign)
        while time.monotonic() < t_end - 2.0:
            trial_deadline = min(t_end, time.monotonic() + trial_slice)
            if time.monotonic() >= trial_deadline:
                break

            use_h = (hseq is not None and float(rng.random()) < 0.22)
            if use_h:
                p = _downsample_mass(hseq, n_proto)
            else:
                strat = int(rng.integers(0, 20))
                p = _make_initial(n_proto, strat, rng)

            c = _conv_square(p)
            sc = float(_score_normalized(p, c))
            _update_best(best_state, p, sc, final_len)
            _add_candidate(proto_cands, p, sc)

            # main exact coordinate polish
            base_rem = trial_deadline - time.monotonic()
            coord_deadline = min(trial_deadline - 6.0,
                                 time.monotonic() + max(3.0, 0.52 * base_rem))
            p, c, sc = _coordinate_optimize(
                p, c, sc, coord_deadline, rng, grid, refine_count, max_perturb=3
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                _update_best(best_state, p, sc, final_len)
                _add_candidate(proto_cands, p, sc)

            # mirror escape, especially useful on moderate prototypes
            if n_proto <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < trial_deadline - 10.0:
                rem = trial_deadline - time.monotonic()
                mir_deadline = min(trial_deadline - 6.0,
                                   time.monotonic() + min(35.0, 0.22 * rem))
                p, c, sc = _mirror_polish(
                    p, c, sc, mir_deadline, rng, max_iters=800
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    _update_best(best_state, p, sc, final_len)
                    _add_candidate(proto_cands, p, sc)

            # LP direction on smaller prototypes
            if n_proto <= 384 and time.monotonic() < trial_deadline - 8.0:
                rem = trial_deadline - time.monotonic()
                lp_deadline = min(trial_deadline - 5.0,
                                  time.monotonic() + min(45.0, 0.24 * rem))
                p, c, sc = _lp_polish(
                    p, c, sc, lp_deadline, max_iters=2, max_n=384
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    _update_best(best_state, p, sc, final_len)
                    _add_candidate(proto_cands, p, sc)

            # final coordinate cleanup before storing
            if time.monotonic() < trial_deadline - 1.0:
                p, c, sc = _coordinate_optimize(
                    p, c, sc, trial_deadline - 1.0, rng, grid, refine_count,
                    max_perturb=1
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    _update_best(best_state, p, sc, final_len)
                    _add_candidate(proto_cands, p, sc)

        if n_proto % 128 == 0 or n_proto <= 24:
            print(f"proto n={n_proto} best={best_state[1]:.8f}", flush=True)

    print(f"after prototypes best={best_state[1]:.8f} t={time.monotonic()-start_time:.1f}s", flush=True)

    # ---------------- Expansion to final length ----------------
    expand_deadline = min(deadline - 220.0, time.monotonic() + 120.0)
    proto_cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    for cand in proto_cands[:14]:
        if time.monotonic() >= expand_deadline:
            break
        sc, nproto, pproto = cand
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or nproto <= 0:
            continue
        pexp = _expand_to_length(pproto, final_len)
        scexp = _score_array(pexp)
        _update_best(best_state, pexp, scexp, final_len)
        _try_full_candidate(full_cands, pexp, scexp, final_len)

    # Full-length diverse starts.
    full_start_deadline = min(deadline - 180.0, time.monotonic() + 80.0)
    full_strategies = [0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 10, 12, 13, 14, 16, 17, 18]
    for st in full_strategies:
        if time.monotonic() >= full_start_deadline:
            break
        try:
            pf = _make_initial(final_len, st, rng)
            cf = _conv_square(pf)
            scf = float(_score_normalized(pf, cf))
            _update_best(best_state, pf, scf, final_len)
            _try_full_candidate(full_cands, pf, scf, final_len)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_state[2] is None and best_state[0] is not None:
        pf = _expand_to_length(best_state[0], final_len)
        scf = _score_array(pf)
        _update_best(best_state, pf, scf, final_len)
        _try_full_candidate(full_cands, pf, scf, final_len)

    full_cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    print(f"before full polish best_full={best_state[3]:.8f} best_any={best_state[1]:.8f}", flush=True)

    # ---------------- Full-length polish ----------------
    full_deadline = deadline - 25.0
    full_grid = _make_grid(18, -9.0, 0.0)
    top_full = full_cands[:5]

    for fi, fcand in enumerate(top_full):
        if time.monotonic() > full_deadline:
            break
        fsc, fload, fseq = fcand
        if fload != final_len or fseq is None:
            continue

        remaining = max(30.0, full_deadline - time.monotonic())
        slice_budget = min(remaining / max(1, len(top_full) - fi), 700.0)
        start = time.monotonic()

        p = fseq.copy()
        c = _conv_square(p)
        sc = float(_score_normalized(p, c))

        mirror_deadline = min(full_deadline - 90.0, start + 0.52 * slice_budget)
        try:
            p, c, sc = _mirror_polish(
                p, c, sc, mirror_deadline, rng, max_iters=4000
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            _update_best(best_state, p, sc, final_len)
            _try_full_candidate(full_cands, p, sc, final_len)

        coord_deadline = min(full_deadline - 10.0, start + slice_budget - 5.0)
        try:
            p, c, sc = _selective_coordinate_polish(
                p, c, sc, coord_deadline, rng, full_grid,
                max_updates=int(max(500, 2 * slice_budget))
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            _update_best(best_state, p, sc, final_len)
            _try_full_candidate(full_cands, p, sc, final_len)

        print(
            f"full polish {fi} old={fsc:.8f} new={sc:.8f} any={best_state[1]:.8f} "
            f"full={best_state[3]:.8f}",
            flush=True
        )

    # ---------------- Final selection ----------------
    final_cands = []

    def _add_unique_final(seq, sc):
        if seq is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        arr = _normalize(seq).copy()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        final_cands.append([float(sc), arr])

    if best_state[0] is not None:
        _add_unique_final(best_state[0], best_state[1])
    if best_state[2] is not None:
        _add_unique_final(best_state[2], best_state[3])
    if hseq is not None:
        _add_unique_final(hseq, _score_array(hseq))
    if best_state[0] is not None and best_state[0].size != final_len:
        _add_unique_final(_expand_to_length(best_state[0], final_len),
                          _score_array(best_state[0]))

    for cand in full_cands[:6]:
        sc, _, seq = cand
        _add_unique_final(seq, sc)

    if not final_cands:
        final_cands.append([float("inf"), _two_level(final_len)])

    final_cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    chosen = None
    chosen_sc = float("inf")
    for sc, seq in final_cands[:10]:
        try:
            asc = _eval_external(seq)
            if np.isfinite(asc) and asc < chosen_sc:
                chosen_sc = asc
                chosen = seq
        except Exception:
            pass

    if chosen is None:
        chosen = (final_cands[0][1] if final_cands else _two_level(final_len))

    # Prefer a full-length construction if it is essentially tied; this keeps
    # the reported construction in the same spirit as the previous runs.
    if best_state[2] is not None:
        try:
            sc_full = _eval_external(best_state[2])
            if np.isfinite(sc_full) and sc_full <= chosen_sc + 0.015:
                chosen = best_state[2]
                chosen_sc = sc_full
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = _normalize(chosen)
    print(f"final local={chosen_sc:.8f} size={out.size}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
